<script>
        $(document).ready(function() {
            function toggleMobileNav() {
                if ( $('.nav-hamburger svg').hasClass('fa-bars') ) {
                    $('.nav-hamburger').html('<i class="fas fa-times"></i>');
                } else {
                    $('.nav-hamburger').html('<i class="fas fa-bars"></i>');
                    $('.nav-mobile-menu').removeClass('col-0').addClass('col');
                    $('.nav-mobile-products').removeClass('col').addClass('col-0');
                    $('.nav-mobile-partners').removeClass('col').addClass('col-0');
                    $('.nav-mobile-more').removeClass('col').addClass('col-0');
                    $('.nav-mobile-regions').removeClass('col').addClass('col-0');
                }
                if ( $('#redirect_notice').hasClass('redirect-notice-active') ) {
                    $('#redirect_notice').stop().slideToggle();
                }
                if ( $('#top_notice').hasClass('top-notice-active') ) {
                    $('#top_notice').stop().slideToggle();
                }
                $('body').toggleClass('overflow-hidden');
                $('.nav-hamburger').toggleClass('nav-hamburger-active');
                $('.nav-mobile-close').toggleClass('nav-mobile-close-active');
                $('.nav-mobile').stop().slideToggle();
                 
            }

            $('.nav-hamburger, .nav-mobile-close').click(function(){ toggleMobileNav(); });
            $('.nav-mobile-products-link').click(function(){ $('.nav-mobile-menu, .nav-mobile-products').toggleClass('col col-0'); });
            $('.nav-mobile-partners-link').click(function(){ $('.nav-mobile-menu, .nav-mobile-partners').toggleClass('col col-0'); });
            $('.nav-mobile-more-link').click(function(){ $('.nav-mobile-menu, .nav-mobile-more').toggleClass('col col-0'); });
            $('.nav-mobile-regions-link').click(function(){ $('.nav-mobile-menu, .nav-mobile-regions').toggleClass('col col-0'); });

            if ( $(document).width() < 768 ) {
                $('.footer-end-links .nav-regions-link, .cart-region-notice .nav-regions-link').click(function(){
                    toggleMobileNav();
                    $('.nav-mobile-menu, .nav-mobile-regions').toggleClass('col col-0');
                });
            } else {
                $('#nav-products-link, .nav-products-hidden').on('touchstart', function(e) {
                    e.preventDefault();

                    if ( !$('#nav-products-link').hasClass('active') ) {
                        navProduct('open');
                    } else {
                        navProduct('close');
                    }
                }).on('mouseenter', function() {
                    navProduct('open');
                }).on('mouseleave', function() {
                    navProduct('close');
                });

                $(document).on('click', '.nav-regions-link', function(e){
                    $('.nav-regions-hidden').css('height','auto');
                    $('.nav-regions-hidden').stop().slideToggle(200);
                    $('.nav-regions-hidden').toggleClass('nav-shadow');
                    $('.nav-regions-link').toggleClass('active');
                    $('.nav-main').toggleClass('detrans-active');
                }).on('click', function(e) {
                    if (!$(e.target).closest('.nav-regions-link, .nav-regions-hidden').length) {
                        if( $('.nav-regions-hidden').is(':visible') ) {
                            $('.nav-regions-hidden').stop().slideUp(200);
                            $('.nav-regions-hidden').removeClass('nav-shadow');
                            $('.nav-regions-link').removeClass('active');
                            $('.nav-main').removeClass('detrans-active');
                        }
                    }
                });
            }

            function navProduct(e) {
                if ( e == 'open' ) {
                    $('.nav-products-hidden').css('height','auto');
                    $('.nav-products-hidden').stop().slideDown(200);
                    $('.nav-products-hidden').addClass('nav-shadow');
                    $('#nav-products-link').addClass('active');
                } else {
                    $('.nav-products-hidden').stop().slideUp(200);
                    $('.nav-products-hidden').removeClass('nav-shadow');
                    $('#nav-products-link').removeClass('active');
                }
            }

            
        });
    </script>

<div class="col-12 col-md-8 header">
<h5 class="fluid-h5">How Our Chairs Are Made</h5>
<p class="fluid-type-lg">To give you uncompromising safety and quality, each product is made of industrial-grade raw materials, manufactured using state of the art processes, which is then subject to rigorous international quality tests.</p>
</div>
<div class="col-12 col-sm-10 col-lg-12 wrapper">
<div class="row content">
<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 order-lg-12 p-0">
<img alt="Secretlab PRIME™ 2.0 PU Leather, Cold-cured high density foam &amp; Carbon structural steel." class="lazy lazy-fade" data-src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/2124/8169/t/141/assets/home-design-material-min.jpg?69032" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 1000 550'%3E%3C/svg%3E"/>
</div>
<div class="col bg-mild body">
<h6 class="fluid-h6">Top-of-the-line Core Materials</h6><br/>
<p class="fluid-type"><b>Upholstery</b><br/> Secretlab PRIME™️ 2.0 PU leather greatly exceeds industry standards for abrasion and flexing resistance</p>
<p class="fluid-type"><b>Foam</b><br/> Secretlab cold-cure foam mix is manufactured with a patent-pending process</p>
<p class="fluid-type"><b>Aluminum</b><br/> ADC12 reinforced Japanese aluminum</p>
<p class="fluid-type" style="margin:0;"><b>Steel</b><br/> Robot-welded carbon structural steel as the foundation</p>
</div>
</div>
<div class="row content">
<div class="col-12 col-lg-6 p-0">
<img alt="Drafts and skecthes of Secretlab design" class="lazy lazy-fade" data-src="//cdn.shopify.com/s/files/1/2124/8169/t/141/assets/home-design-certification-min.jpg?69032" src="data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 1000 550'%3E%3C/svg%3E"/>
</div>
<div class="col bg-mild body">
<h6 class="fluid-h6">Designed and Certified for Safety</h6><br/>
<p class="fluid-type"><b>Gas spring</b><br/> Class 4 heavy duty KGS gas pistons. Safety certifications include: TÜV LGA, ANSI/BIFMA(by SGS) and ISO9001.</p>
<p class="fluid-type"><b>Tilt mechanism</b><br/> Certified for strength and durability according to ANSI/BIFMA and ISO standards.</p>
<p class="fluid-type"><b>Secretlab chairs</b><br/> Each Secretlab chair is sent for ANSI/BIFMA testing, where they are subjected to over 20 strength, durability, and functional tests.</p>

            ];
            
            // SHUFFLE ARRAY FUNCTION
            function shuffle(array) {
                var currentIndex = array.length, temporaryValue, randomIndex;
                // While there remain elements to shuffle...
                while (0 !== currentIndex) {
                    // Pick a remaining element...
                    randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * currentIndex);
                    currentIndex -= 1;
                    // And swap it with the current element.
                    temporaryValue = array[currentIndex];
                    array[currentIndex] = array[randomIndex];
                    array[randomIndex] = temporaryValue;
                }
                return array;
            }
            
            // SOCIALS REVIEWS
            generate_twitter_post(shuffle(tt_review_array));
            function generate_twitter_post(arr) {
                $.each(arr, function (i, v) {
                    $('.reviews-tt-content').append('<div class="reviews-tt"><div class="row no-gutters"><a href="'+v[0]+'" class="col-12" target="_blank" rel="noopener" aria-label="Link to tweet"><div class="twitter-image"><img data-src="'+v[6]+'" class="lazy lazy-fade" alt="'+v[2]+'\s Twitter Photo"></div></a></div>'+
                    '<a href="'+v[0]+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" aria-label="Link to tweet"><div class="row twitter-row-override"><div class="col-auto"><img data-src="'+v[1]+'" alt="'+v[2]+'\s Twitter Profile Image" class="lazy lazy-fade"/></div><div class="col"><span class="name" data-note="user-display-name">'+v[2]+'</span><br><span class="handle" data-note="twitter-at-username">'+v[3]+'</span></div><div class="col reviews-tt-logo text-right"><i class="fab fa-twitter"></i></div></div></a>'+
                    '<div class="row twitter-row-override-2"><div class="col-12"><p data-note="twitter-content">'+v[4]+'</p></div><div class="col-12"><span class="twitter_time">'+generate_time_string(new Date(v[5]), 'twitter', '')+'</span></div></div></div>');
                });
            }
            
            generate_facebook_post(shuffle(fb_review_array));
            function generate_facebook_post(arr) {
                $.each(arr, function (i, v) {
                    $('.reviews-fb-content').append('<div class="reviews-fb"><div class="row"><div class="col-12 review-header"><img data-src="'+v[1]+'" style="float:left" alt="Facebook Profile Image" class="lazy lazy-fade profile-img"/><div class="name" style="float:left">'+v[2]+'<br>'+generate_time_string(new Date(v[4]), 'facebook', v[0])+'</div>'+
                    '<span style="float:right"><a href="'+v[0]+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" aria-label="Link to Facebook post"><i class="fab fa-facebook-square"></i></a></span></div>'+
                    '<div class="col-12 review-img"><a href="'+v[0]+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" aria-label="Link to Facebook post"><img data-src="'+v[5]+'" alt="Facebook Photo" class="lazy lazy-fade"></a></div>'+
                    '<div class="col-12 review-body"><p>'+v[3]+'</p></div></div></div>');
                });
            }
            
            $(document).ready(function() {
                $('.reviews-tt-content').slick({
                    infinite: true,
                    arrows: true,
                    prevArrow: '<div class="option-prev"><i class="fas fa-angle-left"></i></div>',
                    nextArrow: '<div class="option-next"><i class="fas fa-angle-right"></i></div>',
                    speed: 500,
                    slidesToShow: 1,
                    focusOnSelect: true,
                    initialSlide: 1,
                    adaptiveHeight: true
                })
                $('.reviews-fb-content').slick({
                    infinite: true,
                    arrows: true,
                    prevArrow: '<div class="option-prev"><i class="fas fa-angle-left"></i></div>',
                    nextArrow: '<div class="option-next"><i class="fas fa-angle-right"></i></div>',
                    speed: 500,
                    slidesToShow: 1,
                    focusOnSelect: true,
                    initialSlide: 1,
                    adaptiveHeight: true
                })
            });
            
            function generate_time_string(ts, platform, url) {
                var month_string = ['Jan', 'Feb', 'Mar', 'Apr', 'May', 'Jun', 'Jul', 'Aug', 'Sep', 'Oct', 'Nov', 'Dec'],
                    month_date = month_string[ts.getMonth()] + ' ' + ts.getDate(),
                    month_date_year = month_date + ', ' + ts.getFullYear(),
                    hh = ts.getHours(),
                    mm = ts.getMinutes(),
                    am = ' AM';
                if (hh > 11) { am = ' PM'; }
                if (hh > 12) { hh -= 12; }
                if (mm < 10) { mm = '0'+mm; }
                var time_string = hh+':'+mm+am;
                if ('facebook' === platform) {
                    return '<a class="timestamp" href="'+url+'" title="'+month_date_year+' at '+time_string+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener">'+month_date+'</a>';
                } else if ('twitter' === platform) {
                    return time_string+' - '+month_date_year;
                }
            }
        </script>

                var star_full = '<svg class="fa-c-star" version="1.1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 50 50" style="enable-background:new 0 0 50 50;" xml:space="preserve"><polygon style="fill:#FFC107" points="25,1.33 32.69,17.91 49.88,18.41 37.44,31.54 40.38,48.67 25,40.58 9.62,48.67 12.56,31.54 0.12,18.41 17.31,17.91 "></polygon></svg>';
                var star_half = '<svg class="fa-c-star-half" version="1.1" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" xmlns:xlink="http://www.w3.org/1999/xlink" x="0px" y="0px" viewBox="0 0 50 50" style="enable-background:new 0 0 50 50;" xml:space="preserve"><polygon style="fill:#FFC107" points="25,1.33 17.31,17.91 0.12,18.41 12.56,31.54 9.62,48.67 25,40.58 "></polygon><g><path style="fill:#FFC107" d="M25,7.28l5.42,11.69l0.65,1.4l1.55,0.04l11.61,0.34l-8.6,9.07l-0.86,0.91l0.21,1.23l2.08,12.13l-10.89-5.73L25,37.76l-1.16,0.61L12.94,44.1l2.08-12.13l0.21-1.23l-0.86-0.91l-8.6-9.07l11.61-0.34l1.55-0.04l0.65-1.4L25,7.28 M25,1.33l-7.69,16.58l-17.19,0.5l12.44,13.13L9.62,48.67L25,40.58l15.38,8.09l-2.94-17.12l12.44-13.13l-17.19-0.5L25,1.33L25,1.33z"></path></g></svg>';
                
                generate_stamped_reviews(stamped_reviews);
                function generate_stamped_reviews(arr) {
                    var selected = shuffle(arr).slice(0,7);
                    $.each(selected, function (i,v) {
                        var addStars = star_full.repeat(Math.floor(v[4])); if ( v[4] % 1 == 0.5 ) { addStars += star_half }; 
                        $('#stamped_review_slick').append('<div class="index-reviews-post"><div class="review-stars">'+ addStars +'</div><div class="review-author">'+ v[2] +'<span class="review-verified" data-toggle="tooltip" data-placement="top" title="Verified Buyer"><i class="fas fa-check-circle"></i></span></div><div class="review-country">'+ v[3] +'</div><div class="review-body">'+ v[5] +'</div><a href="/pages/reviews?stamped_r_id='+ v[1] +'" class="review-link">Read full review<img data-src="'+ v[0] +'" alt="'+ v[2] +'\'s Review Image" class="review-img lazy lazy-fade"></a></div>');
                    });
                }
                function shuffle(array) {
                    var currentIndex = array.length, temporaryValue, randomIndex;
                    // While there remain elements to shuffle...
                    while (0 !== currentIndex) {
                        // Pick a remaining element...
                        randomIndex = Math.floor(Math.random() * currentIndex);
                        currentIndex -= 1;
                        // And swap it with the current element.
                        temporaryValue = array[currentIndex];
                        array[currentIndex] = array[randomIndex];
                        array[randomIndex] = temporaryValue;
                    }
                    return array;
                }
                
                $(function () {
                    $('#stamped_review_slick').slick({
                        prevArrow: '<button type="button" aria-label="Previous" class="review-prev"></button>',
                        nextArrow: '<button type="button" aria-label="Next" class="review-next"></button>',
                        infinite: false,
                        autoplay: false,
                        slidesToShow: 1,
                        initialSlide: 2,
                        centerMode: true,
                        centerPadding: '5.25vw',
                        mobileFirst: true,
                        adaptiveHeight: true,
                        responsive: [
                            {
                                breakpoint: 600,
                                settings: {
                                    slidesToShow: 2,
                                    centerPadding: '5.25vw'
                                }
                            },
                            {
                                breakpoint: 991,
                                settings: {
                                    slidesToShow: 3,
                                    centerPadding: '8.5vw',
                                    initialSlide: 1
                                }
                            },
                            {
                                breakpoint: 1299,
                                settings: {
                                    slidesToShow: 4,
                                    centerPadding: '8.5vw',
                                    initialSlide: 1
                                }
                            },
                            {
                                breakpoint: 1599,
                                settings: {
                                    slidesToShow: 4,
                                    centerPadding: '16.6vw',
                                    initialSlide: 3
                                }
                            }
                        ]
                    });
                    $('.review-verified').tooltip()
                });
            </script>
